| portalShader | "shader_type canvas_item;\nuniform float preview_time = 0.0;\nuniform vec4 tint : source_color = vec4(0.27,0.17,0.35,1.0);\nvoid fragment(){ vec2 p=(UV-vec2(0.5))*vec2(2.0,1.4); float d=length(p); float bands=step(0.52,fract(d*5.0-preview_time*0.35)); float a=step(d,0.75)*step(0.18,d); COLOR=vec4(tint.rgb*(0.65+0.35*bands),a*tint.a); }\n" |

The275map IDs above remain actual Sprite2D metadata identities. Runtime clearing IDs are the independent exact left/right.clearing.1/.2/.3 each suffixed.state.0/.state.1;decorative stump IDs are left/right.prop.4/.9/.14. Portal runtime IDs expand all six exact portal IDs with.front,.back,.interior,.blocker and.ruin.0through.ruin.12. Sprite-bearing clip layers expand with.clip.index and clipID.index native Animation identities,all source layers enumerated above. Minimap expands known portal IDs andP1/P2/P3/P4;defense markers follow exact knownDefenses in reveal samples. Persistent loop resources use effect.ID with captured source and current.firelight;explicit transient effects use effect.ID plus.marker/.outline where declared. Root WorldPreview metadata carries sample ID/epoch. These identities are native resource observations,not native-policy grants.
